## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 gpu-6000pro-2 的 cuda:0 和 cuda:1（项目配置白名单）
- OLMo 模型路径必须用 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/olmo-1b-hf/`（不是 olmo-1b）
- submit_training_job 环境激活用 `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`（不是 .venv）
- 跨策略泛化 claim 已降级为 "strategy-dependent"（D004），不能再说 "strategy-agnostic"
- DCOL 是 secondary contribution，Atlas/EDD 是 primary（D002 framing 决策）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：gpu-6000pro-2（AutoDL，2× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 98GB，aml-train 镜像）
- 工作目录：`/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/`
- Python：`/root/miniconda3/bin/python`，conda base 环境
- 模型路径：`/root/autodl-tmp/models/` 下有 pythia-1.4b/, gpt2-xl/, olmo-1b-hf/, deberta-v3-large/
- HF_HOME：`/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache`
- 活跃进程：dcol_deberta_001 (PID 193684, cuda:0, tmux 管理)
- Cron：dcol_deberta_001 有活跃 cron 监控

### 3. 📊 当前状态
刚收到 gpt2xl_temp09_001 完成回调（ANALYZING），已派 Worker 分析结果（worker_analysis_gpt2xl_temp09_001_20260514T222101）。
同时派了 Worker 准备并提交 olmo_temp09_001 到 cuda:1（worker_code_olmo_temp09_001_20260514T222144）。

Registry 活跃实验：
- `dcol_deberta_001` RUNNING cuda:0 — DeBERTa-v3-large + Contrastive Ordinal Loss，epochs=10
- `gpt2xl_temp09_001` ANALYZING — GPT-2 XL temp_only τ=0.9，等 Worker 分析
- `olmo_temp09_001` QUEUED — OLMo-1B temp_only τ=0.9，Worker 正在准备提交

等待中：
- Worker 回调：analysis worker（gpt2xl_temp09_001 结果）+ code worker（olmo_temp09_001 提交）
- dcol_deberta_001 cron 回调（训练中，约 10 epochs）

### 4. 📋 待办
- analysis worker 回调 → 读 metrics，update_registry(conclusion=success/negative)，更新 report.md，汇报 Director（带 [资源] 行 + vs baseline 对比）
- code worker 回调 → 确认 olmo_temp09_001 已提交（RUNNING），更新 ROADMAP
- dcol_deberta_001 cron 完成 → 分析 DCOL vs RF baseline，这是论文核心实验
- 3×3 矩阵剩余 cells（OLMo topk50, GPT-2 XL topk50）→ cuda 空出后排队
- gpt2xl_temp09_001 如果 success → 3×3 矩阵 7/9 完成，向 Director 汇报进度

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- 项目核心：递归生成深度估计——文本经过多轮 model→data→model 后可从统计特征区分深度
- D001: MVP PASS 78.2%（Pythia nucleus）→ 进入 research phase
- D002: Dream 审稿后 framing 重心从 DCOL 转向 Atlas/EDD
- D003: Baselines 统一用 DeBERTa-v3-large 消除模型规模混淆
- D004: 跨策略 claim 降级为 "strategy-dependent"（topk50 比 nucleus 低 8.6pp）
- 3×3 矩阵趋势：GPT-2 XL > OLMo > Pythia；nucleus > temp09 > topk50
- 易错点：topk50 信号弱不代表 negative——69.6% 仍远超 chance(25%)，只是强度降级
- DCOL 是关键实验——如果 DCOL 超 RF baseline，论文 story 更强；如果不超，需要调整 framing 让 Atlas/EDD 独立成立

## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)

## 约束

- Qwen2.5 系列代码占比过高，LoRA 无法覆盖，已验证不可用
- 根分区 ~30GB，模型/数据必须在 `/root/autodl-tmp/`
- OLMo 必须用 `olmo-1b-hf` 目录（不是 `olmo-1b`）
- DCOL margin_lr 不能超过 1e-4（1e-3 导致 embedding collapse）
- OLMo cron 监控有 false failure 风险（子进程 PID vs pipeline 主进程）
- OpenAI SDK base_url: 代码无需修改，v1.0+ 自动读取 OPENAI_BASE_URL 环境变量

前 12 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。

Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. |\\\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\\\n\\\\n### Analysis\\\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold.   "task_prompt": "你是 ML 研究执行 Worker。任务：对 3×3 递归生成深度估计矩阵做综合分析，产出分析报告。\n\n## 背景\n项目研究递归生成深度估计——文本经过多轮 model→data→model 后可从统计特征区分深度。已完成 3 模型 × 3 解码策略的 8/9 矩阵格子（gpt2xl_topk50 仍在运行）。\n\n## 已完成矩阵数据（4-class RF accuracy）\n\n| 模型 | nucleus p=0.95 | temp τ=0.9 | top-k k=50 |\n|------|---------------|-----------|-----------|\n| Pythia-1.4B | 78.2% | 76.3% | 69.6% |\n| OLMo-1B | 78.8% | 82.0% | 75.7% |\n| GPT-2 XL | 83.3% | 85.4% | 🔄 running |\n\n## 你的任务\n\n1. 用 `read_registry` 拉取所有 8 个完成实验的完整 metrics（包括 pairwise AUC、3-class、feature importance、code contamination）\n2. 产出综合分析报告（Markdown），写入项目 artifacts 目录，内容：\n   a. 完整 3×3 矩阵表格（4-class acc、d1v2 AUC、3-class filtered acc）\n   b. 模型效应分析：GPT-2 XL vs OLMo vs Pythia，跨策略平均\n   c. 策略效应分析：nucleus vs temp09 vs topk50，跨模型平均\n   d. 交互效应：哪些模型-策略组合表现异常（如 GPT-2 XL temp09 超过 nucleus）\n   e. Feature importance 稳定性：top-5 features 在 8 个 cells 中的一致性\n   f. Code contamination 模式：不同模型/策略的 contamination 率对比\n   g. 关键 findings 摘要（3-5 条，可直接用于论文）\n3. 用 `register_artifact` 注册产出文件\n\n## 产出路径\n- 分析报告：`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/artifacts/matrix_3x3_analysis.md`\n\n## 注意\n- 所有数字必须来自 registry，不要假设\n- 标注每个数字的来源实验 ID\n- 对比时用 percentage points (pp) 不用百分比变化",

}

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
The registry entry for dcol_deberta_003 doesn't have server/gpu in the right format. Let me update the spec with the proper server and gpu info, then try again.

Looking at the registry entry from earlier:
```
"dcol_deberta_003": {
    "status": "PREPARING",  // now ALLOCATED
    "spec": {
        "server": "gpu-6000pro-2",
        "gpu": "cuda:0",  // this was the original, but allocate_and_submit should have set it to cuda:1


```

I think the issue is that after allocate_and_submit ran with gpu_ids=["cuda:1"], the status updated to ALLOCATED but the server and gpu fields weren't properly promoted to the top level of the registry entry where the tool expects them. I need to restructure the registry so these fields sit at the root level alongside status.
````

  "task_prompt": "你是 ML 研究执行 Worker。任务：清理 stale tmux session 并提交 DCOL v3 训练到 cuda:1。\n\n## 背景\ndcol_deberta_003 是 DeBERTa-v3-large + Contrastive Ordinal Loss (DCOL) 实验的第三次尝试。v1 因 embedding collapse 失败（margin_lr 太高），v2 因 OOM 失败（与其他实验共享 GPU）。v3 修复了超参（margin_lr=1e-4, L2 norm, cl_weight=1.0）且独占 cuda:1。\n\n## 问题\n之前的提交尝试创建了一个 stale tmux session \"dcol_deberta_003\"，导致重复提交失败。\n\n## 步骤\n\n1. SSH 到 gpu-6000pro-2（用 ssh_execute），清理 stale tmux session：\n   ```\n   tmux kill-session -t dcol_deberta_003 2>/dev/null; echo \"cleaned\"\n   ```\n\n2. 验证 cuda:1 是空闲的：\n   ```\n   nvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,memory.used,memory.total --format=csv,noheader\n   ```\n   确认 GPU 1 的显存使用接近 0。\n\n3. 用 `submit_training_job` 提交实验：\n   - exp_id: dcol_deberta_003\n   - server: gpu-6000pro-2\n   - gpu_ids: [\"cuda:1\"]\n   - command: `bash -c \"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est && python src/dcol_trainer.py --data_dir data/ --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/models/deberta-v3-large --features_path data/features_all.jsonl --output_dir checkpoints/dcol_v3/ --batch_size 32 --epochs 10 --lr 2e-5 --margin_lr 1e-4 --cl_weight 1.0 --seed 42\"`\n   - work_dir: /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\n\n4. 验证实验已启动：\n   ```\n   tmux list-sessions\n   nvidia-smi\n   ```\n\n5. 用 update_registry 补充 spec 信息（如果 submit_training_job 没自动补）：\n   - checkpoint_path: /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/checkpoints/dcol_v3/\n   - log_path: 从 submit_training_job 返回的 log_path\n\n## 环境信息\n- 服务器：gpu-6000pro-2（AutoDL，2× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 98GB）\n- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python，conda base 环境\n- 工作目录：/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/\n- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/models/deberta-v3-large\n- 数据：data/features_all.jsonl（20000 samples，4 depths × 5000）\n\n## 注意\n- 只使用 cuda:1，绝不动 cuda:0（gpt2xl_topk50_001 在上面跑）\n- 如果 cuda:1 上有任何进程，先报告情况，不要 kill\n- output_dir 用 checkpoints/dcol_v3/（不是 dcol_v2/，避免与旧实验冲突）",

{
  "task_type": "code:dcol_deberta_003_submit",
  "project": "recursive_gen_depth_est",
  "task_prompt": "你是 ML 研究执行 Worker。任务：清理 stale tmux session 并提交 DCOL v3 训练到 cuda:1。\n\n## 背景\ndcol_deberta_003 是 DeBERTa-v3-large + Contrastive Ordinal Loss (DCOL) 实验的第三次尝试。v1 因 embedding collapse 失败（margin_lr 太高），v2 因 OOM 失败（与其他实验共享 GPU）。v3 修复了超参（margin_lr=1e-4, L2 norm, cl_weight=1.0）且独占 cuda:1。\n\n## 问题\n之前的提交尝试创建了一个 stale tmux session \"dcol_deberta_003\"，导致重复提交失败。\n\n## 步骤\n\n1. SSH 到 gpu-6000pro-2（用 ssh_execute），清理 stale tmux session：\n   ```\n   tmux kill-session -t dcol_deberta_003 2>/dev/null; echo \"cleaned\"\n   ```\n\n2. 验证 cuda:1 是空闲的：\n   ```\n   nvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,memory.used,memory.total --format=csv,noheader\n   ```\n   确认 GPU 1 的显存使用接近 0。\n\n3. 用 `submit_training_job` 提交实验：\n   - exp_id: dcol_deberta_003\n   - server: gpu-6000pro-2\n   - gpu_ids: [\"cuda:1\"]\n   - command: `bash -c \"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est && python src/dcol_trainer.py --data_dir data/ --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/models/deberta-v3-large --features_path data/features_all.jsonl --output_dir checkpoints/dcol_v3/ --batch_size 32 --epochs 10 --lr 2e-5 --margin_lr 1e-4 --cl_weight 1.0 --seed 42\"`\n   - work_dir: /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\n\n4. 验证实验已启动：\n   ```\n   tmux list-sessions\n   nvidia-smi\n   ```\n\n5. 用 update_registry 补充 spec 信息（如果 submit_training_job 没自动补）：\n   - checkpoint_path: /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/checkpoints/dcol_v3/\n   - log_path: 从 submit_training_job 返回的 log_path\n\n## 环境信息\n- 服务器：gpu-6000pro-2（AutoDL，2× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 98GB）\n- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python，conda base 环境\n- 工作目录：/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/\n- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/models/deberta-v3-large\n- 数据：data/features_all.jsonl（20000 samples，4 depths × 5000）\n\n## 注意\n- 只使用 cuda:1，绝不动 cuda:0（gpt2xl_topk50_001 在上面跑）\n- 如果 cuda:1 上有任何进程，先报告情况，不要 kill\n- output_dir 用 checkpoints/dcol_v3/（不是 dcol_v2/，避免与旧实验冲突）\n- dcol_deberta_003 当前 registry 状态是 QUEUED（之前的 ALLOCATED 已回滚）",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh",
    "registry"
  ]
}
